该系统评价遵循 PRISMA 2020 声明,并前瞻性地在 PROSPERO 中注册(CRD420251132701)。
证据基础与方法
检索了 PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus 和 Embase 的系统综述,包括荟萃分析和伞式综述,调查 SARS-CoV-2 相关微血管血栓形成与急性肾损伤之间的关联。
共识与结论
微血管或血栓事件与不良肾脏结局相关(OR 2.14;95%CI 1.32-3.48)。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据没有明确指出争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
这些发现强化了内皮功能障碍和血栓炎症途径与 COVID-19 期间肾脏受累的相关性,并强调了对高危患者进行早期肾脏监测、风险分层和肾脏保护策略的必要性。

Microvascular Thrombosis and Acute Kidney Injury in COVID-19: A Systematic Review and Quantitative Analysis

Authors: Carolyne AC Duarte, Vanessa Schnekenberg Martins Uscocovich, Ivo Misael, Príscila Diane Assed Caires DUARTE, Eduarda Baranselli Sestito, Pollyanna Nicolly da Silva, Duarte PDAC, Duarte, C. A., Uscocovich, V. S. M., Duarte, P. D. A. C.
Original Abstract
Abstract Objective: To synthesize the available evidence on the association between SARS-CoV-2-related microvascular thrombosis and acute kidney injury (AKI), with emphasis on renal outcomes, mortality, and renal replacement therapy requirements. Methods: This systematic review followed the PRISMA 2020 statement and was prospectively registered in PROSPERO (CRD420251132701). PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, and Embase were searched for systematic reviews, including meta-analyses, and umbrella reviews investigating the association between SARS-CoV-2-related microvascular thrombosis and acute kidney injury. Two reviewers independently performed study selection, data extraction, and methodological quality assessment using AMSTAR-2 and ROBIS. Evidence was synthesized through a structured narrative synthesis supported by quantitative data extracted from the included reviews. Results: Six evidence syntheses evaluating kidney involvement, thrombotic events, and microvascular mechanisms in COVID-19 were included. AKI incidence was 9.2% (95%CI 4.6-13.9) among hospitalized patients and 32.6% (95%CI 8.5-56.6) among critically ill patients. In children with multisystem inflammatory syndrome associated with SARS-CoV-2, AKI incidence was 20% (95%CI 14-28). Microvascular or thrombotic events were associated with adverse renal outcomes (OR 2.14; 95%CI 1.32-3.48). AKI was associated with increased mortality (OR 4.68; 95%CI 1.06-20.70) and greater likelihood of renal replacement therapy requirement (OR 2.87; 95%CI 1.45-5.68). The certainty of evidence ranged from moderate to high for the principal outcomes. Conclusion: Current evidence supports an important association between microvascular thrombotic injury and COVID-19-associated AKI. These findings reinforce the relevance of endothelial dysfunction and thromboinflammatory pathways in kidney involvement during COVID-19 and highlight the need for early renal monitoring, risk stratification, and kidney-protective strategies in high-risk patients. Keywords: COVID-19; Acute Kidney Injury; Microvascular Thrombosis; SARS-CoV-2; Renal Replacement Therapy; Systematic Review
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review followed the PRISMA 2020 statement and was prospectively registered in PROSPERO (CRD420251132701).
Evidence base and method
PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, and Embase were searched for systematic reviews, including meta-analyses, and umbrella reviews investigating the association between SARS-CoV-2-related microvascular thrombosis and acute kidney injury.
Consensus and conclusions
Microvascular or thrombotic events were associated with adverse renal outcomes (OR 2.14; 95%CI 1.32-3.48).
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
These findings reinforce the relevance of endothelial dysfunction and thromboinflammatory pathways in kidney involvement during COVID-19 and highlight the need for early renal monitoring, risk stratification, and kidney-protective strategies in high-risk patients.

COVID-19大流行的间接肿瘤负担:肺癌诊断、手术管理和医疗保健系统恢复力的全球性破坏——叙述性回顾

The indirect oncological burden of the COVID-19 pandemic: global disruptions in lung cancer diagnosis, surgical management, and healthcare system resilience — a narrative review
作者： Rasih Yazkan, Hasan Ekrem Çamaş, Süleyman Emre Akın, S. E. Akin
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 2019 年冠状病毒病(COVID-19)大流行给全球医疗保健系统带来了巨大挑战,显着影响了肺癌患者的诊断、治疗过程和临床结果。本文旨在评估大流行的间接肿瘤学后果,特别强调从胸外科角度诊断、分期和手术治疗的延误。方法 在大流行期间,医院实施了广泛的结构和行政改革,包括重组临床服务、分配专门的 COVID-19 病房、推迟择期手术以及重新分配医疗资源。虽然这些措施对于控制感染至关重要,但它们极大地限制了获得常规肿瘤护理的机会。本研究旨在作为叙述性回顾。在 PubMed 数据库中检索了 2020 年 1 月至 2026 年 2 月期间发表的研究文献,其中使用了术语“COVID-19”、“肺癌”、“胸外科”、“延迟诊断”的组合。其中包括评估大流行对肺癌诊断、分期、治疗和结果影响的英文文章。根据临床相关性和方法学质量选择相关研究。作为排除标准,病例报告被排除在研究之外。结果 来自多项国际研究的证据表明,肺癌诊断数量大幅减少、手术量减少、转诊和治疗间隔延长以及就诊时疾病阶段向更晚期的转变。筛查中断、患者不愿就医以及诊断程序受限导致了全球“诊断缺陷”。尽管在应用适当的保护方案时,手术结果总体上仍然是安全的,但治疗延迟和治疗策略的改变,包括增加放射治疗和全身治疗的使用,引起了人们对长期肿瘤结果的担忧。总体而言,这场大流行凸显了全球健康危机期间癌症护理系统的脆弱性,同时也表明可以通过结构化方案和专用途径维持胸部肿瘤服务的连续性。结论 这些发现强调了开发有弹性的医疗保健系统的必要性,该系统能够在未来的大流行期间保持及时的癌症诊断和治疗,以最大限度地减少肺癌患者的间接死亡率和生命年损失。

The indirect oncological burden of the COVID-19 pandemic: global disruptions in lung cancer diagnosis, surgical management, and healthcare system resilience — a narrative review

Authors: Rasih Yazkan, Hasan Ekrem Çamaş, Süleyman Emre Akın, S. E. Akin
Original Abstract
Abstract Background The coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic imposed substantial challenges on healthcare systems worldwide, significantly influencing the diagnosis, treatment processes, and clinical outcomes of patients with lung cancer. This article aims to evaluate the indirect oncological consequences of the pandemic, with particular emphasis on delays in diagnosis, staging, and surgical treatment from a thoracic surgery perspective. Methods During the pandemic, extensive structural and administrative transformations were implemented in hospitals, including reorganization of clinical services, allocation of dedicated COVID-19 units, postponement of elective procedures, and redistribution of healthcare resources. While these measures were essential for infection control, they significantly limited access to routine oncological care. This study was designed as a narrative review. Literature was searched in PubMed databases for studies published between January 2020 and February 2026 using combinations of the terms “COVID-19”, “lung cancer”, “thoracic surgery”, “delay diagnosis”. English-language articles evaluating the impact of the pandemic on lung cancer diagnosis, staging, treatment, and outcomes were included. Relevant studies were selected based on clinical relevance and methodological quality. Case reports were excluded from the study as exclusion criteria. Results Evidence from multiple international studies demonstrates substantial reductions in lung cancer diagnoses, decreased surgical volumes, prolonged referral and treatment intervals, and a shift toward more advanced disease stages at presentation. Screening interruptions, patient reluctance to seek medical attention, and restricted diagnostic procedures contributed to a global “diagnostic deficit.” Although surgical outcomes remained generally safe when appropriate protective protocols were applied, treatment delays and altered therapeutic strategies, including increased use of radiotherapy and systemic therapies, raised concerns regarding long-term oncological outcomes. Overall, the pandemic highlighted the vulnerability of cancer care systems during global health crises while also demonstrating that continuity of thoracic oncology services can be maintained through structured protocols and dedicated pathways. Conclusion These findings underscore the necessity of developing resilient healthcare systems capable of preserving timely cancer diagnosis and treatment during future pandemics to minimize indirect mortality and loss of life-years among lung cancer patients.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study was designed as a narrative review.
Evidence base and method
English-language articles evaluating the impact of the pandemic on lung cancer diagnosis, staging, treatment, and outcomes were included.
Consensus and conclusions
Results Evidence from multiple international studies demonstrates substantial reductions in lung cancer diagnoses, decreased surgical volumes, prolonged referral and treatment intervals, and a shift toward more advanced disease stages at presentation.
Controversies and gaps
While these measures were essential for infection control, they significantly limited access to routine oncological care.
Research and practice implications
Conclusion These findings underscore the necessity of developing resilient healthcare systems capable of preserving timely cancer diagnosis and treatment during future pandemics to minimize indirect mortality and loss of life-years among lung cancer patients.

COVID-19 时代后甲型 H3N2 流感的基因组和抗原进化:重点关注 J 和 K 亚分支的范围界定审查以及对疫苗有效性的影响

Genomic and Antigenic Evolution of Influenza A(H3N2) After the COVID-19 Era: A Scoping Review with Focus on J and K Subclades and Implications for Vaccine Effectiveness
作者： A. Cianciulli, E. Santoro, S. Esposito, Savino Quaglierella, A. Pacifico, Michele Nappa, Domenico Fornino, Roberta Manente, Mario Capunzo, G. Boccia
摘要中文翻译COVID-19 后,甲型流感 (H3N2) 重新出现,且基因多样化加速。抗原漂移亚支的增加引起了人们对免疫逃逸和疫苗不匹配的担忧。为了综合有关甲型流感 (H3N2) 大流行后演变的现有证据,特别关注新兴分支及其潜在的公共卫生影响,2026 年 1 月对六个数据库进行了范围界定审查 (PRISMA-ScR),包括基因组监测、抗原特征和疫苗有效性 (VE) 的研究。其中包括二十项研究。虽然进化枝 2a.3a.1(J 谱系)占主导地位,但报告强调了抗原独特的 K 亚进化枝(以前的 J.2.4.1)的迅速出现。遗传和实验室检测(HI/中和)证实了免疫逃逸和疫苗匹配减少。观察估计表明,对于漂移菌株,VE 有所减少,但没有观察到临床严重程度的一致增加。大流行后 A(H3N2) 的定义是快速漂移和多样化。K 亚支等新兴变种挑战疫苗选择和季节性准备。综合监测对于及时更新疫苗和减轻公共卫生影响仍然至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了后 COVID-19 时代甲型流感 (H3N2) 的基因组和抗原进化,重点关注 J 和 K 亚支及其对疫苗有效性的影响。核心问题涉及这些分支中加速的遗传多样性和抗原漂移如何影响免疫逃逸和疫苗错配。
证据基础与方法
2026 年 1 月,对六个数据库进行了范围界定审查 (PRISMA-ScR),以综合有关大流行后甲型 H3N2 流感演变的证据,包括基因组监测、抗原特征和疫苗有效性。根据预先确定的资格标准纳入了二十项研究。
共识与结论
大流行后甲型流感 (H3N2) 的特点是快速遗传多样性和 2a.3a.1 分支(J 谱系)的优势,以及抗原独特的 K 亚分支的快速出现。遗传和实验室检测(HI/中和)证实了免疫逃逸并减少了漂移毒株的疫苗匹配,观察估计表明疫苗针对这些变异的有效性降低。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了各个分支之间抗原漂移和免疫逃逸的异质性,对于疫苗匹配减少是否会导致临床严重程度的持续增加存在相互矛盾的发现。此外,证据基础缺乏抗原表征和疫苗有效性估计的标准化方法,限制了研究之间的可比性。
科研与实践启示
像 K 这样的抗原性不同的亚支的出现对季节性疫苗株的选择提出了挑战,并且需要综合基因组和抗原监测来及时更新疫苗。加强监测系统并协调抗原表征和疫苗有效性评估的方法对于减轻公共卫生影响至关重要。

Genomic and Antigenic Evolution of Influenza A(H3N2) After the COVID-19 Era: A Scoping Review with Focus on J and K Subclades and Implications for Vaccine Effectiveness

Authors: A. Cianciulli, E. Santoro, S. Esposito, Savino Quaglierella, A. Pacifico, Michele Nappa, Domenico Fornino, Roberta Manente, Mario Capunzo, G. Boccia
Original Abstract
Post-COVID-19, influenza A(H3N2) has re-emerged with accelerated genetic diversification. The rise in antigenically drifted subclades raises concerns regarding immune escape and vaccine mismatch. To synthesize the available evidence on the post-pandemic evolution of influenza A(H3N2), with particular attention to emerging subclades and their potential public health implications, a scoping review (PRISMA-ScR) was conducted across six databases in January 2026, including studies on genomic surveillance, antigenic characterization, and vaccine effectiveness (VE). Twenty studies were included. While clade 2a.3a.1 (J lineage) predominated, reports highlighted the rapid emergence of the antigenically distinct K subclade (formerly J.2.4.1). Genetic and laboratory assays (HI/neutralization) confirmed immune escape and reduced vaccine match. Observational estimates indicated diminished VE against drifted strains, though no consistent increase in clinical severity was observed. Post-pandemic A(H3N2) is defined by rapid drift and diversification. Emerging variants like the K subclade challenge vaccine selection and seasonal preparedness. Integrated surveillance remains vital for timely vaccine updates and mitigating public health impact.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the genomic and antigenic evolution of influenza A(H3N2) in the post-COVID-19 era, focusing on the J and K subclades and their implications for vaccine effectiveness. The central question addresses how accelerated genetic diversification and antigenic drift in these subclades may impact immune escape and vaccine mismatch.
Evidence base and method
A scoping review (PRISMA-ScR) was conducted across six databases in January 2026 to synthesize evidence on post-pandemic influenza A(H3N2) evolution, including genomic surveillance, antigenic characterization, and vaccine effectiveness. Twenty studies were included based on predefined eligibility criteria.
Consensus and conclusions
Post-pandemic influenza A(H3N2) is characterized by rapid genetic diversification and the predominance of clade 2a.3a.1 (J lineage), alongside the rapid emergence of the antigenically distinct K subclade. Genetic and laboratory assays (HI/neutralization) confirmed immune escape and reduced vaccine match for drifted strains, with observational estimates indicating diminished vaccine effectiveness against these variants.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights heterogeneity in antigenic drift and immune escape across subclades, with conflicting findings on whether reduced vaccine match translates to consistent increases in clinical severity. Additionally, the evidence base lacks standardized methods for antigenic characterization and vaccine effectiveness estimation, limiting comparability across studies.
Research and practice implications
The emergence of antigenically distinct subclades like K challenges seasonal vaccine strain selection and necessitates integrated genomic and antigenic surveillance to inform timely vaccine updates. Strengthening surveillance systems and harmonizing methodologies for antigenic characterization and vaccine effectiveness assessment are critical to mitigate public health impact.

COVID-19 大流行期间秘鲁亚马逊地区难以到达的土著社区的疟疾负担和护理:一项混合方法研究

Malaria burden and care in hard-to-reach indigenous communities in the Peruvian Amazon during the COVID-19 pandemic: A mixed-methods study
作者： Fernández SP, L. M. Rojas, Gómez J, J. Sandoval-Bances, S. Condori-Catachura, García PD, S. Abrams, S. Anthierens, H. Bastiaens, S. M. Chenet
摘要中文翻译背景 据报道,在疟疾流行的偏远土著社区,获得医疗服务的机会有限,因此 COVID-19 大流行与医疗服务提供和疟疾控制活动中断有关。由于疟疾监测、诊断和治疗的中断,秘鲁亚马逊地区的偏远土著社区可能特别容易受到影响。我们调查了 COVID-19 大流行对秘鲁亚马逊地区难以到达的土著社区的疟疾负担、医疗保健获取和社区认知的影响。在疟疾流行地区,这些教训尤其重要,因为未来的突发卫生事件可能同样影响监测、诊断、预防和治疗活动,有可能逆转通过疟疾控制计划取得的成果。方法 2021 年和 2022 年在秘鲁亚马逊地区里奥圣地亚哥区的四个土著社区进行了一项收敛并行混合方法研究。定量数据通过横断面疟疾调查收集,定性数据通过对居民、社区卫生工作者和当地卫生人员的半结构化访谈获得。通过显微镜检测疟疾感染并通过PCR确认。基于广义估计方程(GEE)方法研究了与疟疾感染发生相关的因素,该方法应用于聚类二元结果数据,并考虑了研究年份中参与者的重复观察结果。对定性数据进行了主题分析,并使用支柱集成流程框架集成了这两个组件。结果 共有 514 名参与者被纳入。基于 PCR 的疟疾患病率在 2021 年为 17.6%,在 2022 年为 26.09%。与其他研究社区相比,Alianza Progreso 社区的恶性疟疾患病率特别高(2021 年:24.19%,2022 年:16.16%)。既往疟疾感染和发冷与 PCR 确诊的疟疾感染有关。定性研究结果表明,与 COVID-19 相关的干扰影响了疟疾控制活动,因为中断了诊断和治疗的可用性、减少了社区随访以及将 COVID-19 应对活动置于疟疾控制之上。参与者还描述了对 COVID-19 的恐惧、暂时迁移到农场地区以及在大流行期间对草药的依赖。结论 COVID-19 大流行可能加剧了秘鲁亚马逊偏远土著社区疟疾控制方面先前存在的挑战。加强疟疾监测、确保诊断和治疗的连续性以及改善地理偏远社区的医疗保健服务对于支持秘鲁消除疟疾工作至关重要。作者摘要 COVID-19 大流行扰乱了全世界的基本卫生服务,可能对医疗服务有限的偏远疟疾流行社区造成更大的影响。由于疟疾监测、诊断、治疗和控制活动的中断,秘鲁亚马逊地区的土著居民面临着额外的挑战。我们通过将疟疾筛查与对社区成员、社区卫生工作者和医护人员的访谈相结合,评估了 COVID-19 大流行对秘鲁亚马逊地区四个偏远土著社区的疟疾负担和医疗保健获取的影响。我们的研究结果表明,疟疾在大流行期间持续传播,特别是在受恶性疟原虫影响的社区。参与者报告说,获得疟疾诊断和治疗的机会减少,社区后续活动减少,害怕去医疗机构就诊,以及就医行为发生变化。这些发现凸显了突发卫生事件如何加剧地理偏远人群中的现有障碍,并威胁消除疟疾的努力。加强有复原力的卫生系统并确保未来危机期间基本疟疾服务的连续性对于保护亚马逊地区的脆弱社区至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查研究了 COVID-19 大流行对秘鲁亚马逊地区难以到达的土著社区的疟疾负担、医疗保健获取和社区认知的影响,重点关注大流行期间对疟疾控制活动的干扰和持续传播。
证据基础与方法
该研究于 2021 年和 2022 年在里奥圣地亚哥地区的四个土著社区采用了收敛并行混合方法设计,将横断面疟疾调查与对居民、社区卫生工作者和当地卫生人员的半结构化访谈相结合。通过显微镜检测疟疾感染并通过 PCR 确认,并使用广义估计方程 (GEE) 方法分析因素。
共识与结论
基于 PCR 的疟疾患病率从 2021 年的 17.6% 增加到 2022 年的 26.09%,恶性疟原虫传播持续存在,特别是在 Alianza Progreso 社区。既往疟疾感染和发冷与 PCR 确诊的疟疾感染有关。
争议与缺口
该审查没有报告标准化效应大小、汇总估计或跨社区的比较分析。定性研究结果表明诊断和治疗受到干扰,但这些影响在不同环境中的程度和一致性仍不清楚。
科研与实践启示
加强疟疾监测、确保诊断和治疗的连续性以及改善地理偏远社区的医疗保健服务对于支持秘鲁消除疟疾工作至关重要,特别是在突发卫生事件期间。

Malaria burden and care in hard-to-reach indigenous communities in the Peruvian Amazon during the COVID-19 pandemic: A mixed-methods study

Authors: Fernández SP, L. M. Rojas, Gómez J, J. Sandoval-Bances, S. Condori-Catachura, García PD, S. Abrams, S. Anthierens, H. Bastiaens, S. M. Chenet
Original Abstract
Background The COVID-19 pandemic was associated with reported disruptions to healthcare delivery and malaria control activities in remote, malaria-endemic indigenous communities with limited access to healthcare. Remote indigenous communities in the Peruvian Amazon may have been especially vulnerable due to interruptions in malaria surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment. We investigated the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on malaria burden, healthcare access, and community perceptions in hard-to-reach indigenous communities in the Amazonas region of Peru. In malaria-endemic regions, these lessons are particularly important because future health emergencies may similarly affect surveillance, diagnosis, prevention, and treatment activities, potentially reversing gains achieved through malaria control programs. Methods A convergent parallel mixed-methods study was conducted in 2021 and 2022 across four indigenous communities in the Río Santiago district, Amazonas region, Peru. Quantitative data were collected through cross-sectional malaria surveys, and qualitative data were obtained through semi- structured interviews with inhabitants, community health workers, and local health personnel. Malaria infections were detected by microscopy and confirmed by PCR. Factors associated with the occurrence of malaria infection were studied based on a generalized estimating equation (GEE) approach applied to clustered binary outcome data and accounting for repeated observations within participants across study years. Qualitative data were analyzed thematically, and both components were integrated using the Pillar Integration Process framework. Results A total of 514 participants were included. PCR-based malaria prevalence was 17.6% in 2021 and 26.09% in 2022. falciparum prevalence was particularly high in the Alianza Progreso community (2021: 24.19%, 2022: 16.16%) compared to the other studied communities. Previous malaria infection and chills were associated with PCR-confirmed malaria infection. Qualitative findings indicated that COVID-19-related disruptions affected malaria control activities by interrupting diagnosis and treatment availability, reducing community follow-up, and prioritizing COVID-19 response activities over malaria control. Participants also described fear of COVID-19, temporary migration to farm areas, and reliance on herbal medicine during the pandemic. Conclusions The COVID-19 pandemic may have contributed to exacerbated pre-existing challenges to malaria control in remote indigenous communities in the Peruvian Amazon. Strengthening malaria surveillance, ensuring continuity of diagnosis and treatment, and improving healthcare access in geographically isolated communities will be critical to supporting malaria elimination efforts in Peru. Author summary The COVID-19 pandemic disrupted essential health services worldwide, with potentially greater consequences for remote malaria-endemic communities with limited healthcare access. Indigenous populations in the Peruvian Amazon faced additional challenges due to interruptions in malaria surveillance, diagnosis, treatment, and control activities. We assessed the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on malaria burden and healthcare access in four remote indigenous communities in the Amazonas region of Peru by combining malaria screening with interviews involving community members, community health workers, and healthcare personnel. Our findings showed persistent malaria transmission during the pandemic period, particularly in communities affected by Plasmodium falciparum . Participants reported reduced access to malaria diagnosis and treatment, fewer community follow-up activities, fear of attending health facilities, and changes in healthcare-seeking behaviors. These findings highlight how health emergencies can intensify existing barriers in geographically isolated populations and threaten malaria elimination efforts. Strengthening resilient health systems and ensuring continuity of essential malaria services during future crises will be critical to protect vulnerable communities in the Amazon region.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on malaria burden, healthcare access, and community perceptions in hard-to-reach indigenous communities in the Amazonas region of Peru, focusing on disruptions to malaria control activities and persistent transmission during the pandemic period.
Evidence base and method
The study employed a convergent parallel mixed-methods design in 2021 and 2022 across four indigenous communities in the Río Santiago district, combining cross-sectional malaria surveys with semi-structured interviews of inhabitants, community health workers, and local health personnel. Malaria infections were detected by microscopy and confirmed by PCR, with factors analyzed using a generalized estimating equation (GEE) approach.
Consensus and conclusions
PCR-based malaria prevalence increased from 17.6% in 2021 to 26.09% in 2022, with Plasmodium falciparum transmission persisting, particularly in the Alianza Progreso community. Previous malaria infection and chills were associated with PCR-confirmed malaria infection.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report standardized effect sizes, pooled estimates, or comparative analyses across communities. Qualitative findings suggest disruptions in diagnosis and treatment, but the magnitude and consistency of these impacts across settings remain unclear.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening malaria surveillance, ensuring continuity of diagnosis and treatment, and improving healthcare access in geographically isolated communities are critical to supporting malaria elimination efforts in Peru, particularly during health emergencies.

COVID-19 大流行期间社会背景下的体力活动:潜在转变分析

Physical activity in social contexts during the COVID-19 pandemic: a latent transition analysis
作者： Nerida Koert van der Linden, Matthew Bourke, Patricia Tucker, Jennifer D. Irwin, Shauna M. Burke
摘要中文翻译背景 虽然在 COVID-19 大流行期间人们对身体活动行为进行了探索,但人们对危机期间发生身体活动的社会背景知之甚少。这项纵向研究的目的是探讨加拿大安大略省 COVID-19 大流行的头两年年轻人(15-24 岁)参与的与身体活动相关的社会环境。具体目标包括确定:(1)社会环境中的参与模式如何随时间变化;(2) 与社会背景相关的阶层成员资格的人口统计/描述性预测因素。方法 在线调查完成时间为:(1)2020年7月-9月;(2)2020年11月-2021年1月;(3)2021年7月-2022年1月。收集性别、生活状况、身体活动以及与身体活动相关的社会背景的数据。进行潜在转变分析以确定参与者的社会背景如何随时间变化,并使用卡方检验来评估潜在概况成员资格的预测因素。结果 参与者(n = 328;M 年龄 = 22.95)被分为三个潜在类别:1 = 主要单独活动;2 = 单独活动。2 = 在所有情况下体力活动均有限;3 = 主要与他人交往。在时间 1,近 70.0% 的参与者属于 1 类;在第 2 次和第 3 次,大多数参与者(分别为 62.1% 和 53.6%)属于第 2 类。从第 1-2 次,大多数参与者(67.1%)从第 1 类过渡到第 2 类。从第 2-3 次,超过 75.0% 的参与者留在各自的班级。班级成员资格与不同时间点的各种预测因素相关。结论 结果强调需要促进参与各种与身体活动相关的社会环境,特别是在危机时期。

共识与结论
班级成员资格与不同时间点的各种预测因素相关。

Physical activity in social contexts during the COVID-19 pandemic: a latent transition analysis

Authors: Nerida Koert van der Linden, Matthew Bourke, Patricia Tucker, Jennifer D. Irwin, Shauna M. Burke
Original Abstract
Background While physical activity behaviours have been explored during the COVID-19 pandemic, little is known about the social contexts in which physical activity took place during this time of crisis. The purpose of this longitudinal study was to explore the physical activity-related social contexts in which young people (aged 15-24) engaged during the first two years of the COVID-19 pandemic in Ontario, Canada. Specific objectives included identifying: (1) how patterns of engagement in social contexts changed over time; and (2) demographic/descriptive predictors of social context-related class membership. Methods Online surveys were completed from: (1) July-September 2020; (2) November 2020-January 2021; and (3) July 2021-January 2022. Data on gender, living situation, physical activity, and physical activity-related social contexts were collected. Latent transition analysis was conducted to determine how participant's social contexts changed over time and chi-squared tests were used to assess predictors of latent profile membership. Results Participants (n = 328; M age = 22.95) were categorized into three latent classes: 1 = mainly active alone; 2 = limited physical activity in all contexts; and 3 = mainly active with others. At Time 1, nearly 70.0% of participants were in Class 1; at Times 2 and 3, most participants (62.1% and 53.6%, respectively) were in Class 2. From Times 1-2, most participants (67.1%) transitioned from Class 1 to Class 2. From Times 2-3, more than 75.0% of participants remained in their respective classes. Class membership was associated with various predictors at different time points. Conclusion Results underscore the need to promote participation in a variety of physical activity-related social contexts, particularly during times of crisis.

Background While physical activity behaviours have been explored during the COVID-19 pandemic, little is known about the social contexts in which physical activity took place during this time of crisis.
Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions
Class membership was associated with various predictors at different time points.
Controversies and gaps
Results Participants (n = 328; M age = 22.95) were categorized into three latent classes: 1 = mainly active alone; 2 = limited physical activity in all contexts; and 3 = mainly active with others.
Research and practice implications
Conclusion Results underscore the need to promote participation in a variety of physical activity-related social contexts, particularly during times of crisis.

COVID-19 大流行和 2015 年至 2023 年癌症筛查差异的变化:NSHAP 调查研究。

The COVID-19 Pandemic and Changes in Cancer Screening Disparities from 2015 to 2023: An NSHAP Survey Study.
作者： Lahari Vuppaladhadiam, K. Wroblewski, Phil Schumm, Jasmin A. Tiro, Louise C. Hawkley, Martha K McClintock, Elbert S. Huang
摘要中文翻译背景从1990年到2020年,美国的癌症筛查率稳步上升,但差距仍然存在。癌症筛查因 COVID-19 大流行而中断,可能会加剧差距。目的 我们利用符合年龄条件的成年人的纵向队列来检查大流行前(2015-16)和大流行晚期(2021-23)的癌症筛查率,以评估健康差异。设计 这是一项针对 2015-16 和 2021-23 年度完成国家社会生活、健康和老龄化项目 (NSHAP) 调查的参与者的研究。我们使用单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析来衡量年龄、性别、种族和民族、教育水平、婚姻状况和收入在结直肠癌、前列腺癌和乳腺癌筛查方面的差异。参与者 我们研究了完成两项调查的 2,585 名 NSHAP 参与者的数据。主要指标 分别通过结肠镜检查、乳房 X 光检查和前列腺特异性抗原 (PSA) 检测完成自我报告的过去一年结直肠癌、乳腺癌和前列腺癌筛查率。主要结果 完成结肠镜检查、乳房 X 光检查和 PSA 测试的受访者比例在不同调查中没有变化。较年轻的个体(年龄 < 60 岁)在 2015-16 年的乳房 X 光检查率较低(52.8% vs. 62.9%,p = 0.04),两项调查中的 PSA 检测率也较低(37.0% vs. 53.5%,p < 0.001 和 34.3% vs. 西班牙裔受访者在 2015-16 年的结肠镜筛查率最低(12.1%,p = 0.006)和 2021-23 年(7.6%,p = 0.002),并且在两项调查中,非伴侣男性的 PSA 检测率均低于有伴侣男性(p = 0.02 和 p = 0.005),而 2015-16 年黑人女性的乳房 X 光检查率最高(65.3%,p = 0.04)。2015-16 年(全部 p < 0.05)。结论 2015-16 年至 2021-23 年总体人口癌症筛查率保持一致,没有任何亚组受到 COVID-19 大流行的影响较大。结直肠癌筛查率。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究使用纵向队列研究了美国符合年龄条件的成年人的癌症筛查率,以评估大流行前(2015-16)和大流行后期(2021-23)之间的健康差异。
证据基础与方法
该研究对 2015-16 和 2021-23 完成国家社会生活、健康和老龄化项目 (NSHAP) 调查的参与者进行了基于调查的分析,利用单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析来衡量年龄、性别、种族和民族、教育水平、婚姻状况和收入的差异。
共识与结论
从 2015-16 年到 2021-23 年,总体人口癌症筛查率保持一致,在整个调查中完成结肠镜检查、乳房 X 光检查和 PSA 检测的受访者比例没有显着变化。
争议与缺口
该研究没有发现随着时间的推移,筛查率存在显着的人口统计学差异,但差异仍然存在,西班牙裔人的结直肠癌筛查率持续较低,非伴侣男性的 PSA 检测低于有伴侣的男性。
科研与实践启示
需要针对结直肠癌筛查率持续较低的西班牙裔等人口亚群体进行有针对性的宣传。

The COVID-19 Pandemic and Changes in Cancer Screening Disparities from 2015 to 2023: An NSHAP Survey Study.

Authors: Lahari Vuppaladhadiam, K. Wroblewski, Phil Schumm, Jasmin A. Tiro, Louise C. Hawkley, Martha K McClintock, Elbert S. Huang
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND From 1990 to 2020, cancer screening rates in the United States steadily rose, but disparities persisted. Cancer screening was disrupted by the COVID-19 pandemic, possibly worsening disparities. OBJECTIVE We utilized a longitudinal cohort of age-eligible adults to examine pre-pandemic (2015-16) and late-pandemic (2021-23) cancer screening rates to evaluate health disparities. DESIGN This is a study of participants who completed National Social Life, Health, and Aging Project (NSHAP) surveys in 2015-16 and 2021-23. We used univariable and multivariable logistic regression analyses to measure differences by age, gender, race and ethnicity, education level, marital status, and income in screening for colorectal, prostate and breast cancer. PARTICIPANTS We studied data from 2,585 NSHAP participants who completed both surveys. MAIN MEASURES Self-reported past-year colorectal, breast, and prostate cancer screening rates completed with colonoscopies, mammograms, and prostate-specific antigen (PSA) testing, respectively. KEY RESULTS The proportion of respondents completing colonoscopies, mammograms, and PSA testing did not change across surveys. Younger individuals (aged < 60) had lower mammogram rates in 2015-16 (52.8% vs. 62.9%, p = 0.04) and lower PSA testing in both surveys (37.0% vs. 53.5%, p < 0.001 and 34.3% vs. Hispanic respondents had the lowest colonoscopy screening rates in 2015-16 (12.1%, p = 0.006) and 2021-23 (7.6%, p = 0.002), and Black women had the highest for mammograms in 2015-16 (65.3%, p = 0.04). Non-partnered men had lower PSA testing than partnered men in both surveys (p = 0.02 and p = 0.005). All screening rates were lower for those with lower incomes in 2015-16 (p < 0.05 for all). Analyses did not show significant demographic differences in screening rates over time. CONCLUSIONS Overall population cancer screening rates remained consistent from 2015-16 to 2021-23. Demographic differences in cancer screening rates persisted, with no subgroups disproportionately affected by the COVID-19 pandemic. Targeted outreach is needed for demographic subgroups such as Hispanic individuals with persistently low colorectal cancer screening rates.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study examines cancer screening rates among age-eligible adults in the United States using a longitudinal cohort to evaluate health disparities between pre-pandemic (2015-16) and late-pandemic (2021-23) periods.
Evidence base and method
The study is a survey-based analysis of participants who completed National Social Life, Health, and Aging Project (NSHAP) surveys in 2015-16 and 2021-23, utilizing univariable and multivariable logistic regression analyses to measure differences by age, gender, race and ethnicity, education level, marital status, and income.
Consensus and conclusions
Overall population cancer screening rates remained consistent from 2015-16 to 2021-23, with no significant changes in the proportion of respondents completing colonoscopies, mammograms, and PSA testing across surveys.
Controversies and gaps
The study did not find significant demographic differences in screening rates over time, but disparities persisted, with Hispanic individuals having persistently low colorectal cancer screening rates and non-partnered men having lower PSA testing than partnered men.
Research and practice implications
Targeted outreach is needed for demographic subgroups such as Hispanic individuals with persistently low colorectal cancer screening rates.

社区参与受访者驱动的抽样:加州太平洋岛民福祉和 COVID-19 经济调查,2024 年。

Community-Engaged Respondent-Driven Sampling: The California Pacific Islander Well-Being and COVID-19 Economic Survey, 2024.

摘要中文翻译描述社区参与与受访者驱动抽样 (RDS) 相结合如何及时记录 COVID-19 对加州夏威夷原住民和太平洋岛民 (NHPI) 成年人口的心理健康和社会经济影响。加州太平洋岛民福祉和 COVID-19 经济调查 (CAPIWAVES) 是 NHPI 社区组织和学术研究人员之间的一项合作,他们于 2024 年 1 月至 5 月对加州的 929 名 NHPI 成年人进行了抽样调查。最初的受访者由来自加州北部、中部和南部的 58 名查莫罗人、斐济人、马绍尔人、夏威夷原住民、萨摩亚人和汤加人的方便样本组成,他们还提到了另外 4 人。与美国社区调查相比,CAPIWAVES RDS 的估计有所不同。我们于 2024 年 6 月在一次在线网络研讨会上向 NHPI 社区成员报告了初步数据发现。2025 年 4 月,我们发布了一份报告,其中包含分析后的 CAPIWAVES 数据,以及社区针对政策、规划和研究提出的建议。尽管数据不具有人口代表性,但社区参与的 RDS 是收集和传播少数群体所需数据的有效方法。(《Am J Public Health》。2026 年 7 月 16 日印刷前在线发布:e1-e12。https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308517)。

Community-Engaged Respondent-Driven Sampling: The California Pacific Islander Well-Being and COVID-19 Economic Survey, 2024.

Authors: Brittany N. Morey, Subica AM, Tanjasiri SP, Corina Penaia, Richard C Chang, Tulua ', Cynthia L. Fok, Shreya Paul, Melenaite Fifita, Christen H Marquez
Original Abstract
To describe how community engagement combined with respondent-driven sampling (RDS) enabled timely documentation of the mental health and socioeconomic impacts of COVID-19 on California's Native Hawaiian and Pacific Islander (NHPI) adult population. The California Pacific Islander Well-Being and COVID-19 Economic Survey (CAPIWAVES) was a collaboration between NHPI community-based organizations and academic researchers who sampled 929 NHPI adults in California from January to May 2024. The initial respondents were composed of a convenience sample of 58 CHamoru, Fijian, Marshallese, Native Hawaiian, Sāmoan, and Tongan respondents in Northern, Central, and Southern California, who referred up to 4 others. Compared with the American Community Survey, CAPIWAVES RDS estimates differed. We reported initial data findings to NHPI community members in June 2024 in an online webinar. In April 2025, we released a report with the analyzed CAPIWAVES data, with community-generated recommendations for policy, programming, and research. Although data were not population-representative, community-engaged RDS was an efficient method for collecting and disseminating needed data for minoritized populations. ( Am J Public Health . Published online ahead of print July 16, 2026:e1-e12. https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308517).

流行病与尼日利亚社会:从天花到 COVID-19 的教训

Epidemics and Nigerian Society: Lessons from Smallpox to COVID-19
作者： James Akpan Ekah, Esin Okon Eminue
摘要中文翻译尼日利亚与流行病的斗争,从殖民时代的天花运动到 COVID-19 大流行,提供了一个世纪以来社会如何在生物医学干预和生活社会经验之间导航的记录。尼日利亚历史上和当代的疾病爆发很少被作为一个相互关联的序列来研究。学术将每种流行病隔离在其自身的学科环境中,扭曲了随时间重复出现的治理、文化和社会反应模式。本文通过对尼日利亚的四个流行病时刻(天花和其他前当代流行病,例如艾滋病毒/艾滋病、2014 年埃博拉疫情和 COVID-19)进行比较历史分析来应对这一挑战。该论文借鉴了殖民时期和后殖民时期的健康记录、政府和多边报告以及同行评审的文献。它追溯了制度遗产、公众对国家的信任以及疾病的文化框架如何重新定义了每一次连续的流行病。分析发现,存在很强的路径依赖性,这是天花和脊髓灰质炎运动期间临时建立的机制。另一方面,社区参与、传统领导、分散监控重新成为尼日利亚最有效干预措施背后的决定性因素。其中值得注意的是埃博拉病毒的遏制,而其侵蚀与艾滋病毒/艾滋病和 COVID-19 应对措施的疲软结果相关。该文件最后提出了将以社区为中心、具有文化素养的治理重新定位到尼日利亚流行病防备工作中的建议。

Epidemics and Nigerian Society: Lessons from Smallpox to COVID-19

Authors: James Akpan Ekah, Esin Okon Eminue
Original Abstract
Nigeria's struggle with epidemic disease, from colonial-era smallpox campaigns to the COVID-19 pandemic, provides a century-long record of how a society has navigated between biomedical intervention and lived social experience. Historical and contemporary disease outbreaks in Nigeria are rarely studied as a connected sequence. Scholarship isolates each epidemic within its own disciplinary environment, distorting patterns of governance, culture, and social response that repeat across time. This paper addressed that challenge through a comparative historical analysis of four epidemic moments in Nigeria which are smallpox and other pre-contemporary epidemics such as, HIV/AIDS, the 2014 Ebola outbreak, and COVID-19. The paper draws on colonial and postcolonial health records, government and multilateral reports, and peer-reviewed literature. It traced how institutional legacies, public trust in the state, and cultural frameworks of illness have redefined each successive epidemic. The analysis found a strong path dependency which is are mechanisms improvised during the smallpox and polio campaigns. On the other hand, community engagement, traditional leadership, decentralized surveillance, reemerged as decisive factors behind Nigeria's most effective interventions. Notable among this was the Ebola containment, while their erosion correlates with weaker outcomes in the HIV/AIDS and COVID-19 responses. The paper closes with recommendations for repositioning community-centred, culturally literate governance into Nigeria's epidemic preparedness strive.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Historical and contemporary disease outbreaks in Nigeria are rarely studied as a connected sequence.
Evidence base and method
The paper draws on colonial and postcolonial health records, government and multilateral reports, and peer-reviewed literature.
Consensus and conclusions
The analysis found a strong path dependency which is are mechanisms improvised during the smallpox and polio campaigns.
Controversies and gaps
Notable among this was the Ebola containment, while their erosion correlates with weaker outcomes in the HIV/AIDS and COVID-19 responses.
Research and practice implications
The paper closes with recommendations for repositioning community-centred, culturally literate governance into Nigeria's epidemic preparedness strive.

关于 CUNY 学生在 COVID-19 大流行之前、期间和之后对消毒剂使用看法的调查

A Survey on the Perception of Disinfectant Use Amongst CUNY Students Before, During and Immediately After the COVID-19 Pandemic
作者： Diane Price Banks, Marissa Claudio, Sasha M. Vergez, Nadine Etienne
摘要中文翻译本研究调查了纽约市立大学 (CUNY) 学生在 COVID-19 大流行高峰之前、期间和之后对消毒剂使用的看法和做法,重点关注唐纳德·特朗普总统于 2020 年 4 月发表的一项有争议的声明(暗示可能使用消毒剂治疗 SARS-CoV-2 感染)后观察到的行为。利用对 441 名纽约市立大学学生的在线调查,收集了四个不同时期的清洁和消毒行为数据:大流行前(2020 年 1 月至 2 月)、大流行高峰期间(2020 年 3 月至 4 月)、大流行高峰之后(2020 年 6 月至 8 月)以及上述总统声明之后。数据模式显示,在大流行高峰期,清洁频率和消毒剂使用量有所增加,但在总统声明发表后显着减少。数据还说明了潜在的危险行为,例如用清洁喷雾剂喷洒身体以及在裸露的皮肤上使用家用清洁剂,尽管这些行为在大流行高峰期后通常会减少。该研究强调了公共卫生沟通的影响以及错误信息在危机期间塑造卫生习惯方面的作用。它强调需要清晰、基于证据的信息传递,以促进安全有效的消毒剂使用,并打击可能导致有害行为的错误信息。这项新颖的研究有助于了解特定学生群体如何应对这一流行病,并为未来的公共卫生干预措施提供见解。

A Survey on the Perception of Disinfectant Use Amongst CUNY Students Before, During and Immediately After the COVID-19 Pandemic

Authors: Diane Price Banks, Marissa Claudio, Sasha M. Vergez, Nadine Etienne
Original Abstract
This study investigated the perceptions and practices of disinfectant use among students at the City University of New York (CUNY) before, during, and after the peak of the COVID-19 pandemic, with a focus on behaviors observed following a controversial statement made by President Donald Trump in April 2020 suggesting the potential use of disinfectants as a treatment for a SARS-CoV-2 infection. Utilizing an online survey of 441 CUNY students, data was collected on cleaning and disinfection behaviors during four distinct periods: pre-pandemic (January–February 2020), during the pandemic’s height (March–April 2020), after the pandemic’s height (June–August 2020), and following the aforementioned presidential statement. Data patterns illustrate an increase in cleaning frequency and disinfectant use during the peak of the pandemic, followed by a notable decrease after the presidential statement. The data also illustrated potentially dangerous practices, such as misting the body with cleaning sprays and using household cleaners on bare skin, though these behaviors generally decreased after the peak of the pandemic. The study highlights the impact of public health communication and the role of misinformation in shaping hygiene practices during a crisis. It underscores the need for clear, evidence-based messaging to promote safe and effective disinfectant use and to counter misinformation that may lead to harmful behaviors. This novel research contributes to the understanding of how a specific student population responded to the pandemic and provides insights for future public health interventions.

COVID-19 感染和疫苗接种时代的 Vogt-Koyanagi-Harada:对当前证据的系统回顾和分析

Vogt-Koyanagi-Harada in the era of COVID-19 infection and vaccination: a systematic review and analysis of current evidence
作者： Nelson Luis Cahuapaza-Gutierrez, Cielo Cinthya Calderon-Hernandez, Tatiana Vanessa Villavicencio-Escudero
摘要中文翻译材料和方法 在PubMed、Scopus、EMBASE和Web of Science进行了文献检索。使用Joanna Briggs Institute工具(JBI)和Newcastle-Ottawa评分(NOS)评估研究的风险偏倚和质量。结果 共纳入21份病例报告,涉及116名患者。其中,66名患者发展为SARS-CoV-2感染的新发VKH并发症,14名复发。关于与COVID-19疫苗接种相关的病例,31例为新发VKH诊断。感染亚组中最常见的临床表现为视力模糊、耳鸣和眼痛,而疫苗接种亚组则以视力模糊、视力丧失和视物变形为主。观察到基于mRNA的疫苗不良事件频率较高,特别是Pfizer-BioNTech(48.4%)。大多数病例发生在第一剂后(51.6%),女性更常见,两组均以双眼受累为主。结论 已报告与SARS-CoV-2感染和COVID-19疫苗接种相关的VKH疾病病例。然而,现有证据仅限于时间关联,无法建立因果关系。系统评价注册 PROSPERO CRD42024522472。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述分析了与 SARS-CoV-2 感染和 COVID-19 疫苗接种相关的 Vogt-Koyanagi-Harada (VKH) 疾病发病的当前可用证据。
证据基础与方法
文献检索是在 PubMed、Scopus、EMBASE 和 Web of Science 中进行的。乔安娜·布里格斯研究所工具 (JBI) 和纽卡斯尔-渥太华评分 (NOS) 用于评估偏倚风险和研究质量。纳入病例报告21例,共116例患者。
共识与结论
其中,66名患者发展为SARS-CoV-2感染的新发VKH并发症,14名复发。关于与COVID-19疫苗接种相关的病例,31例为新发VKH诊断。感染亚组中最常见的临床表现为视力模糊、耳鸣和眼痛,而疫苗接种亚组则以视力模糊、视力丧失和视物变形为主。观察到基于mRNA的疫苗不良事件频率较高,特别是Pfizer-BioNTech(48.4%)。大多数病例发生在第一剂后(51.6%),女性更常见,两组均以双眼受累为主。
争议与缺口
现有证据仅限于时间关联,不允许建立因果关系。
科研与实践启示
该审查注册号为 PROSPERO CRD42024522472。

Vogt-Koyanagi-Harada in the era of COVID-19 infection and vaccination: a systematic review and analysis of current evidence

Authors: Nelson Luis Cahuapaza-Gutierrez, Cielo Cinthya Calderon-Hernandez, Tatiana Vanessa Villavicencio-Escudero
Original Abstract
Purpose Review is synthesized and analyze the current available evidence on the onset of Vogt-Koyanagi-Harada (VKH) disease related with both SARS-CoV-2 infection and COVID-19 vaccination.Materials and methods The literature search was undertaken in PubMed, Scopus, EMBASE and Web of Science. The Joanna Briggs Institute tool (JBI) and Newcastle-Ottawa Score (NOS) were used to assess the risk of bias and quality of the studies.Results Twenty-one case reports were included, with a total of 116 patients. Of these, 66 developed new-onset VKH as a complication of SARS-CoV-2 infection, while 14 relapsed. As for the cases associated with COVID-19 vaccination, 31 corresponded to new VKH diagnoses. The most frequent clinical manifestations in the infection subgroup were blurred vision, tinnitus, and ocular pain, whereas in the vaccination subgroup, blurred vision, vision loss, and metamorphopsia predominated. A higher frequency of adverse events was observed with mRNA-based vaccines, particularly Pfizer-BioNTech (48.4%). Most cases occurred after the first dose (51.6%), with a higher prevalence in females and a predominance of bilateral ocular involvement in both groups.Conclusions Cases of VKH disease have been reported in related with both SARS-CoV-2 infection and COVID-19 vaccination. However, the available evidence is limited to temporal associations, which do not allow for the establishment of a causal relationship.Systematic review registration PROSPERO CRD42024522472.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review analyzes the current available evidence on the onset of Vogt-Koyanagi-Harada (VKH) disease related with both SARS-CoV-2 infection and COVID-19 vaccination.
Evidence base and method
The literature search was undertaken in PubMed, Scopus, EMBASE and Web of Science. The Joanna Briggs Institute tool (JBI) and Newcastle-Ottawa Score (NOS) were used to assess the risk of bias and quality of the studies. Twenty-one case reports were included, with a total of 116 patients.
Consensus and conclusions
Of these, 66 developed new-onset VKH as a complication of SARS-CoV-2 infection, while 14 relapsed. As for the cases associated with COVID-19 vaccination, 31 corresponded to new VKH diagnoses. The most frequent clinical manifestations in the infection subgroup were blurred vision, tinnitus, and ocular pain, whereas in the vaccination subgroup, blurred vision, vision loss, and metamorphopsia predominated. A higher frequency of adverse events was observed with mRNA-based vaccines, particularly Pfizer-BioNTech (48.4%). Most cases occurred after the first dose (51.6%), with a higher prevalence in females and a predominance of bilateral ocular involvement in both groups.
Controversies and gaps
The available evidence is limited to temporal associations, which do not allow for the establishment of a causal relationship.
Research and practice implications
The review is registered as PROSPERO CRD42024522472.

儿科受试者中 Long-COVID-19 的负担:系统回顾和荟萃分析

The Burden of Long-COVID-19 Among Pediatric Subjects: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
作者： Giovanni Cioni, Giovanna Letizia Calò, Borana Kerpaci, Anastasia Troia, N. Gregori, Lamberto Manzoli, M. E. Flacco
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:对成人和儿童的长效新冠肺炎 (LC) 负担的现有估计存在很大差异。初步证据表明,这种不一致可能至少部分是由于在大流行期间采用了多个国际实体提出的不均匀的诊断标准,但迄今为止尚未进行全面评估来量化儿童之间的差异程度。本系统回顾和荟萃分析旨在更新儿科受试者中 LC 的现有流行病学估计,并系统评估可归因于所采用的诊断标准的变异程度。方法:系统性文献检索确定了队列研究,这些研究提供了既往感染过 SARS-CoV-2 的儿童和青少年的 LC 数据。我们计算了(a)比例和(b)头对头荟萃分析(a)以量化LC和LC相关症状的汇总率,以及(b)分别根据选定的人口和/或临床特征评估发生LC的可能性。结果:总共纳入 52 项队列研究和 960,089 名受试者。根据 NIH 或 WHO 诊断标准,LC 合并率为 18.1%(95% CI:13.1-23.6),范围为 16.2% 至 21.6%。LC 患者最常报告疲劳、呼吸系统和神经系统症状。分层分析显示,女性、青少年(相对于儿童)、患有合并症的受试者以及既往患有严重 COVID-19 的受试者(与无症状的原发感染者相比)更有可能发展为 LC(均 p < 0.05)。相比之下,与未接种疫苗的个体相比,接种 ≥1 剂抗 SARS-CoV-2 疫苗的个体发生 LC 的可能性没有显着差异(汇总 OR:0.92;95% CI:0.61-1.41)。结论:虽然可用的估计值可能因所采用的定义而有很大差异,但 LC 影响了相当大比例的儿科人群;因此,迫切需要共同的临床标准和诊断方法。此外,鉴于相关方法学的异质性以及大部分现有文献的质量普遍较差,因此有必要采用标准化方法进行高质量的研究来识别高危人群并指导实施有针对性的后续策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述评估了儿科受试者中 Long-COVID-19 的流行病学负担,重点关注诊断标准的变异性以及 Long-COVID 及其症状的汇总率。
证据基础与方法
这项系统回顾和荟萃分析包括对既往感染过 SARS-CoV-2 的儿童和青少年进行的队列研究。该研究确定了 52 项队列研究和 960,089 名受试者,进行比例和头对头荟萃分析,以量化汇总率并评估风险因素。
共识与结论
儿科受试者中 Long-COVID 的汇总率为 18.1%(95% CI:13.1-23.6)。最常见的是疲劳、呼吸系统和神经系统症状。女性、青少年、患有合并症的受试者以及既往患有严重 COVID-19 的受试者更有可能患上长型 COVID。疫苗接种状态并没有显着影响长新冠肺炎发病的可能性(汇总 OR:0.92;95% CI:0.61–1.41)。
争议与缺口
由于诊断标准不一致,可用的估计值差异很大。纳入文献的方法学异质性和普遍质量较差限制了研究结果的可靠性。需要采用标准化方法进行高质量研究来识别高危人群并指导后续策略。
科研与实践启示
迫切需要共同的临床标准和诊断方法来标准化儿科人群的长新冠病毒评估。应针对高危人群实施有针对性的随访策略,包括女性、青少年以及患有合并症或既往严重 COVID-19 的人群。

The Burden of Long-COVID-19 Among Pediatric Subjects: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis

Authors: Giovanni Cioni, Giovanna Letizia Calò, Borana Kerpaci, Anastasia Troia, N. Gregori, Lamberto Manzoli, M. E. Flacco
Original Abstract
Background/Objectives: There is wide variability in the available estimates on the burden of Long-COVID (LC), both among adults and children. Preliminary evidence suggests that this inconsistency may be due, at least in part, to the adoption of uneven diagnostic criteria, proposed by several international entities during the course of the pandemic, but no comprehensive evaluation has been performed so far to quantify the extent of variation among children. This systematic review and meta-analysis aims to update the available epidemiological estimates of LC among pediatric subjects, and to systematically appraise the degree of variability that can be attributed to the diagnostic criteria adopted. Methods: A systematic literature search identified cohort studies providing data on LC among children and adolescents with a previous SARS-CoV-2 infection. We computed (a) proportion and (b) head-to-head meta-analyses (a) to quantify the pooled rates of LC and LC-related symptoms, and (b) to assess the likelihood of developing LC based upon selected demographic and/or clinical characteristics, respectively. Results: In total, 52 cohort studies and 960,089 subjects were included. The pooled rate of LC was 18.1% (95% CI: 13.1–23.6), ranging from 16.2% to 21.6%, according to NIH or WHO diagnostic criteria, respectively. Fatigue, respiratory and neurological symptoms were most commonly reported by LC patients. Stratified analyses showed that females, adolescents (vs. children), subjects with comorbidities, and those with a previous severe COVID-19 (as compared to asymptomatic primary infections) were more likely to develop LC (all p < 0.05). In contrast, the likelihood of LC onset did not significantly vary between individuals with ≥1 anti-SARS-CoV-2 vaccine dose, versus the unvaccinated (pooled OR: 0.92; 95% CI: 0.61–1.41). Conclusions: Although the available estimates may largely vary depending on the adopted definition, LC affects a meaningful proportion of the pediatric population; as such, common clinical criteria and diagnostic approaches are urgently needed. Moreover, given the relevant methodological heterogeneity and the generally poor-to-fair quality of a large part of the available literature, high-quality studies adopting standardized methodologies are warranted to identify at-risk populations and guide the implementation of targeted follow-up strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review evaluates the epidemiological burden of Long-COVID-19 among pediatric subjects, focusing on the variability attributable to diagnostic criteria and the pooled rates of Long-COVID and its symptoms.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review and meta-analysis included cohort studies on children and adolescents with prior SARS-CoV-2 infection. The study identified 52 cohort studies and 960,089 subjects, performing proportion and head-to-head meta-analyses to quantify pooled rates and assess risk factors.
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled rate of Long-COVID among pediatric subjects was 18.1% (95% CI: 13.1–23.6). Fatigue, respiratory, and neurological symptoms were most commonly reported. Females, adolescents, subjects with comorbidities, and those with prior severe COVID-19 were more likely to develop Long-COVID. Vaccination status did not significantly affect the likelihood of Long-COVID onset (pooled OR: 0.92; 95% CI: 0.61–1.41).
Controversies and gaps
The available estimates vary widely due to inconsistent diagnostic criteria. The methodological heterogeneity and generally poor-to-fair quality of the included literature limit the reliability of the findings. High-quality studies with standardized methodologies are needed to identify at-risk populations and guide follow-up strategies.
Research and practice implications
Common clinical criteria and diagnostic approaches are urgently needed to standardize Long-COVID assessment in pediatric populations. Targeted follow-up strategies should be implemented for at-risk groups, including females, adolescents, and those with comorbidities or prior severe COVID-19.

非编码RNA在SARS-CoV-2感染相关心血管病理中的调控机制和转化应用

The regulatory mechanisms and translational applications of non-coding RNA in SARS-CoV-2 infection-related cardiovascular pathology
作者： Ziwei Cao, Yurong Song, Jing Li, Yuan Cao
摘要中文翻译SARS-CoV-2感染不仅会导致严重的呼吸道疾病,还会对心血管系统产生重大影响,诱发急性心脏损伤和各种长期心血管并发症。非编码RNA(ncRNA)作为基因表达调控的关键分子,在病毒感染和心血管病理过程中表现出重要的调控作用。目前研究表明,miRNA、lncRNA、circRNA等ncRNA通过调节细胞凋亡、代谢重编程和细胞通讯等参与SARS-CoV-2感染相关心脏损伤的发生和发展。然而,其具体的监管网络和临床应用仍然存在许多挑战和未解之谜。该综述系统总结了ncRNA在COVID-19相关心血管疾病中的表达变化和功能特征,重点关注其作为生物标志物和治疗靶点的潜力,并结合最新的多组学数据和前沿技术,预测精准干预和个性化治疗的发展方向。

The regulatory mechanisms and translational applications of non-coding RNA in SARS-CoV-2 infection-related cardiovascular pathology

Authors: Ziwei Cao, Yurong Song, Jing Li, Yuan Cao
Original Abstract
SARS-CoV-2 infection not only leads to severe respiratory diseases but also has a significant impact on the cardiovascular system, inducing acute cardiac injury and various long-term cardiovascular complications. Non-coding RNAs (ncRNAs), as key molecules in gene expression regulation, exhibit important regulatory roles in viral infections and cardiovascular pathological processes. Current research indicates that ncRNAs such as miRNA, lncRNA, and circRNA participate in the occurrence and development of cardiac injury related to SARS-CoV-2 infection by regulating apoptosis, metabolic reprogramming, and cell communication. However, there are still many challenges and unresolved mysteries regarding their specific regulatory networks and clinical applications. This review systematically summarizes the expression changes and functional characteristics of ncRNAs in COVID-19-related cardiovascular diseases, focusing on their potential as biomarkers and therapeutic targets, and combining the latest multi-omics data and cutting-edge technologies to anticipate the development direction of precise interventions and personalized treatments.

大流行如何重塑欧洲呼吸道病毒数据格局:范围界定审查。

How Pandemics Have Reshaped the Respiratory Virus Data Landscape in Europe: Scoping Review.
作者： Brice Mastrovito, Claire Nour Abou Chakra, Cédric Mahé, Marta C Nunes
摘要中文翻译背景:由流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2 引起的急性呼吸道感染仍然是欧洲的主要公共卫生挑战。尽管针对这些病原体的监测系统已经很完善,但在过去的 20 年里,支持监测和研究的数据流迅速多样化。这种不断扩大的数据格局,加上机构、部门和国家之间的碎片化,可能会限制及时的证据综合和有效的公共卫生决策。目的:本次范围界定审查旨在确定和描述过去 20 年来欧洲用于流感、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2 监测和研究的数据源,并检查它们随时间的演变、它们与研究目标的一致性以及数据可用性和使用的地理差异。方法:我们使用目标驱动的分析框架进行了范围界定审查。MEDLINE、Web of Science 和 Embase 确定了 2005 年 1 月至 2025 年 9 月期间发表的实证报告。符合条件的报告重点关注流感、RSV 或 SARS-CoV-2,并包含来自 12 个欧洲国家的数据。临床和介入研究被排除在外。报告根据 4 个研究目标进行分类:流行病学监测、干预措施评估、疾病负担和健康结果评估,以及人口对公共卫生措施的依从性和信任度分析。数据源分为 9 类,包括监测系统、电子健康记录 (EHR)、登记、索赔、调查、数字、环境、综合数据集等。结果:共纳入2564份实证报告。随着时间的推移,呼吸道病毒研究依赖于越来越多样化的数据流。虽然监测系统仍然处于核心地位,特别是流行病学监测,但其相对主导地位有所下降。从 2020 年起,电子病历、登记、索赔数据、数字源以及链接或集成数据集的使用显着扩大,同时开放获取数据的使用也有所增加。数据源的使用因研究目的而异:监测数据在监测和干预评估中占主导地位;危险因素和治疗效果研究中的电子病历;血清流行率调查和公众信任分析;以及经济负担评估中的索赔数据。观察到了巨大的地理差异。北欧国家更频繁地使用链接和多源数据集,而南欧国家更频繁地依赖开放访问或单一来源数据。结论:本次范围界定审查应用创新的目标驱动框架,为欧洲 20 年来呼吸道病毒监测和研究提供了多病原体、跨国数据源映射。与之前专注于单一病原体或数据类型的审查不同,它提供了基于 2564 份报告的综合比较视角,为公共卫生决策提供信息。COVID-19 大流行加速了数据生成和获取方面的创新,但进展仍然主要集中在 SARS-CoV-2 上,而结构碎片化继续限制整个欧洲及时、整合的数据。加强准备工作需要可互操作的基础设施、联合分析平台、可持续的监测创新资金以及跨部门数据共享。

范围与问题
本次范围界定审查旨在确定和描述过去 20 年来欧洲用于流感、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2 监测和研究的数据源,并检查它们随时间的演变、它们与研究目标的一致性以及数据可用性和使用的地理差异。
证据基础与方法
符合条件的报告重点关注流感、RSV 或 SARS-CoV-2,并包含来自 12 个欧洲国家的数据。

从 2020 年起,电子病历、登记、索赔数据、数字源以及链接或集成数据集的使用显着扩大,同时开放获取数据的使用也有所增加。

How Pandemics Have Reshaped the Respiratory Virus Data Landscape in Europe: Scoping Review.

Authors: Brice Mastrovito, Claire Nour Abou Chakra, Cédric Mahé, Marta C Nunes
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Acute respiratory infections caused by influenza, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and SARS-CoV-2 remain a major public health challenge in Europe. Although surveillance systems for these pathogens are well established, the past 2 decades have seen a rapid diversification of data streams supporting surveillance and research. This expanding data landscape, combined with fragmentation across institutions, sectors, and countries, may limit timely evidence synthesis and effective public health decision-making. OBJECTIVE: This scoping review aimed to identify and characterize data sources used for surveillance and research on influenza, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2 in Europe over the past 20 years, and to examine their evolution over time, their alignment with research objectives, and geographic variation in data availability and use. METHODS: We conducted a scoping review using an objective-driven analytical framework. Empirical reports published between January 2005 and September 2025 were identified in MEDLINE, Web of Science, and Embase. Eligible reports focused on influenza, RSV, or SARS-CoV-2 and included data from 12 European countries. Clinical and interventional studies were excluded. Reports were classified according to 4 research objectives: epidemiological monitoring, evaluation of interventions, assessment of disease burden and health outcomes, and analyses of population adherence and trust toward public health measures. Data sources were grouped into 9 categories, including surveillance systems, electronic health records (EHRs), registries, claims, surveys, digital, environmental, integrated datasets, and others. RESULTS: A total of 2564 empirical reports were included. Over time, respiratory virus research relied on an increasingly diverse set of data streams. While surveillance systems remained central, particularly for epidemiological monitoring, their relative dominance declined. From 2020 onward, there was a marked expansion in the use of EHRs, registries, claims data, digital sources, and linked or integrated datasets, alongside increased use of open-access data. Data source use varied by research objective: surveillance data predominated in monitoring and intervention evaluation; EHRs in studies of risk factors and treatment effectiveness; surveys in seroprevalence and public trust analyses; and claims data in assessments of economic burden. Substantial geographic disparities were observed. Northern European countries more frequently used linked and multisource datasets, whereas Southern Europe relied more often on open-access or single-source data. CONCLUSIONS: This scoping review provides a multipathogen, cross-country mapping of data sources for respiratory virus surveillance and research in Europe over 2 decades, applying an innovative objective-driven framework. Unlike prior reviews focused on single pathogens or data types, it offers a consolidated, comparative perspective based on 2564 reports to inform public health decision-making. The COVID-19 pandemic accelerated innovation in data generation and access, but progress remained largely centered on SARS-CoV-2, while structural fragmentation continues to limit timely, integrated data across Europe. Strengthening preparedness will require interoperable infrastructures, federated analysis platforms, sustainable funding for surveillance innovations, and cross-sectoral data sharing.

Scope and question
This scoping review aimed to identify and characterize data sources used for surveillance and research on influenza, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2 in Europe over the past 20 years, and to examine their evolution over time, their alignment with research objectives, and geographic variation in data availability and use.
Evidence base and method
Eligible reports focused on influenza, RSV, or SARS-CoV-2 and included data from 12 European countries.
Consensus and conclusions
From 2020 onward, there was a marked expansion in the use of EHRs, registries, claims data, digital sources, and linked or integrated datasets, alongside increased use of open-access data.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening preparedness will require interoperable infrastructures, federated analysis platforms, sustainable funding for surveillance innovations, and cross-sectoral data sharing.

在一组患有系统性自身免疫性风湿性疾病的患者中,门诊口服抗病毒药物与 COVID-19 28 和 90 天后遗症风险的关系

Association of oral outpatient antiviral medications for COVID-19 with the risk of post-acute sequelae of COVID-19 at 28 and 90 days in a cohort of individuals with systemic autoimmune rheumatic diseases
作者： Madison Negron, Yi-Xiang Wang, Xiaosong Wang, Lauren A. O’Keeffe, Grace Qian, Kevin T. Mueller, Alene A. Saavedra, Natalie A. Davis, Liya S. Getachew, Jeffrey A. Sparks

Association of oral outpatient antiviral medications for COVID-19 with the risk of post-acute sequelae of COVID-19 at 28 and 90 days in a cohort of individuals with systemic autoimmune rheumatic diseases

Authors: Madison Negron, Yi-Xiang Wang, Xiaosong Wang, Lauren A. O’Keeffe, Grace Qian, Kevin T. Mueller, Alene A. Saavedra, Natalie A. Davis, Liya S. Getachew, Jeffrey A. Sparks

SARS-CoV-2 变异和人口因素对儿科患者 COVID-19 严重程度的影响

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

COVID-19 仪表板

COVID-19 dashboard
新闻简报世界卫生组织的 COVID-19 仪表板提供了 2019 年 12 月 31 日至今的确诊病例、死亡、住院、疫苗、变异和废水数据的全球概览。该仪表板每周二更新,汇总来自世卫组织各区域和会员国的信息。自 2023 年 8 月 25 日起,世卫组织已从要求每日报告病例和死亡报告转向加强每周报告。确诊病例的数据定义于 2023 年 7 月 22 日更新,所有数据均需接受回顾性验证、批量更正和可变报告实践,这可能会导致数据激增或负值。该仪表板旨在通过提供每周统计数据来减少视觉上的误解,尽管它承认一些国家已经停止报告或改变了报告频率。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该仪表板显示从 2019 年 12 月 31 日至今的数据,每周更新一次,涵盖前两周(N1、N44、N46)。
地点与对象
全球覆盖世卫组织六个区域(非洲、东地中海、欧洲、美洲区域、东南亚、西太平洋)和 194 个国家/地区(N1、N40)。
事件
WHO COVID-19 仪表板提供确诊病例、死亡、住院、疫苗、变异和废水数据的全球流行病学监测(N1、N4、N5)。
规模、影响与风险
数据包括会员国报告的确诊病例和死亡人数;计数可能会进行回顾性更新、批量更正和可变报告频率,这可能会导致峰值或负值(N8、N9、N19、N20、N32、N33、N34)。
应对、状态与不确定性
自2023年8月25日起,世卫组织不再要求每日报告,而是要求每周报告;“确诊”病例的数据定义于 2023 年 7 月 22 日更新;统计数据基于报告的数据,由于报告差异(N26、N28、N35、N37),可能无法反映实际计数。
A high priorityPublic-health news

COVID-19 dashboard

News Brief
The World Health Organization's COVID-19 dashboard provides a global overview of confirmed cases, deaths, hospitalizations, vaccines, variants, and wastewater data from 31 December 2019 to the present. Updated every Tuesday, the dashboard aggregates information from WHO regions and Member States. As of 25 August 2023, WHO has shifted from requiring daily case and death reports to strengthening weekly reporting. Data definitions for confirmed cases were updated on 22 July 2023, and all figures are subject to retrospective verification, bulk corrections, and variable reporting practices, which may lead to data spikes or negative values. The dashboard aims to mitigate visual misinterpretation by presenting weekly statistics, though it acknowledges that some countries have stopped reporting or changed their frequency.
View five news elements
Time
The dashboard presents data from 31 December 2019 to present, with weekly updates every Tuesday covering the two full weeks prior (N1, N44, N46).
Location and population
Global coverage across WHO's six regions (African, Eastern Mediterranean, European, Region of the Americas, South-East Asia, Western Pacific) and 194 countries/territories (N1, N40).
Event
WHO COVID-19 dashboard providing global epidemiological monitoring of confirmed cases, deaths, hospitalizations, vaccines, variants, and wastewater data (N1, N4, N5).
Scale, impact and risk
Data includes confirmed cases and deaths reported by Member States; counts are subject to retrospective updates, bulk corrections, and variable reporting frequencies, which may cause spikes or negative values (N8, N9, N19, N20, N32, N33, N34).
Response, status and uncertainty
As of 25 August 2023, WHO no longer requires daily reporting and requests weekly reporting; data definitions for 'confirmed' cases were updated on 22 July 2023; statistics are based on reported data and may not reflect actual counts due to reporting differences (N26, N28, N35, N37).

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

纤溶因素:在 COVID-19 的命运中是友还是敌? - 阿尔伯塔大学

Fibrinolytic factors: friends or foes in the fate of COVID-19? - University of Alberta
新闻简报阿尔伯塔大学的研究人员于 2026 年 7 月 20 日发表了一项研究,分析了纤溶因子在重症 COVID-19 中的作用。该研究检测了 120 名住院患者的血纤溶酶原和血管抑制素水平,其中 60 人存活,60 人死亡。研究结果表明,无论潜在的健康状况如何,较低的纤溶酶原水平始终与较高的死亡风险相关。相比之下,血管抑制素水平与生存率表现出复杂的关系,表明低水平和高水平都可能有害,具体取决于患者的合并症负担。该研究提出,确定最佳的血管抑制素水平可以指导个性化治疗,并表明在保留其抗病毒活性的同时阻断血管抑制素的有害作用的肽可能是一种有前途的未来治疗方法。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026 年 7 月 20 日。事件日期:所引用的研究于 2026 年进行,涉及对因 COVID-19 住院的患者进行回顾性分析。
地点与对象
加拿大阿尔伯塔大学。研究人群包括 120 名因 COVID-19 住院的患者:60 名幸存者和 60 名非幸存者。
事件
阿尔伯塔大学发表的一项研究调查了纤溶因子(纤溶酶原和血管抑制素)与重症 COVID-19 死亡率之间的关系。该研究是一项科学分析,而不是疫情报告。
规模、影响与风险
该研究涉及 120 名住院的 COVID-19 患者。研究结果表明,无论合并症如何,血液中纤溶酶原水平较低与死亡风险较高相关。血管抑制素水平与生存存在复杂的、特定于患者的关系,这表明根据患者的健康状况,低水平和高水平都可能有害。该研究没有报告当前疫情的具体病例数或死亡人数。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是一项有初步结果的科学研究。作者认为,确定血管抑制素水平的“最佳点”可以指导个性化治疗。该研究为未来肽的临床前测试奠定了基础,这些肽可以保留血管抑制素的抗病毒活性,同时阻止其有害作用。来源是一所著名大学的完整原始报告。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Fibrinolytic factors: friends or foes in the fate of COVID-19? - University of Alberta

News Brief
Researchers at the University of Alberta published a study on 20 July 2026 analyzing the role of fibrinolytic factors in severe COVID-19. The study examined blood levels of plasminogen and angiostatin in 120 hospitalized patients, 60 of whom survived and 60 who did not. The findings indicate that lower plasminogen levels were consistently associated with a higher risk of death, regardless of underlying health conditions. In contrast, angiostatin levels showed a complex relationship with survival, suggesting that both low and high levels could be harmful depending on the patient's comorbidity burden. The study proposes that identifying an optimal angiostatin level could guide personalized therapies and suggests that peptides blocking angiostatin's harmful effects while preserving its antiviral activity may be a promising future treatment approach.
View five news elements
Time
Publication date: 20 July 2026. Event date: The study referenced was conducted in 2026, involving a retrospective analysis of patients hospitalized for COVID-19.
Location and population
University of Alberta, Canada. The study population consisted of 120 patients hospitalized for COVID-19: 60 survivors and 60 non-survivors.
Event
A research study published by the University of Alberta investigating the relationship between fibrinolytic factors (plasminogen and angiostatin) and mortality in severe COVID-19. The study is a scientific analysis and not an outbreak report.
Scale, impact and risk
The study involved 120 hospitalized COVID-19 patients. The findings indicate that lower blood levels of plasminogen were associated with a higher hazard of death, regardless of comorbidities. Angiostatin levels showed a complex, patient-specific relationship with survival, suggesting that both low and high levels could be harmful depending on the patient's health status. The study does not report specific case counts or deaths for the current outbreak.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study is a scientific investigation with preliminary results. The authors suggest that identifying a 'sweet spot' for angiostatin levels could guide personalized therapies. The research is presented as a foundation for future preclinical testing of peptides that may preserve angiostatin's antiviral activity while blocking its harmful effects. The source is a full original report from a reputable university.
原文 / Source
Published: 2026-07-19  |  Publisher: The Times of India

安得拉邦报告的四例 Covid-19 病例中检测到 Omicron RF.5 亚变体

Omicron RF.5 sub-variant detected in four Covid-19 cases reported in Andhra Pradesh
新闻简报2026 年 7 月 19 日,《印度时报》报道称,安得拉邦卡达帕区的 4 例 Covid-19 病例中检测到了 Omicron RF.5 亚变种。这些样本被送往浦那国家病毒学研究所进行基因组测序。卫生官员澄清,RF.5 是 JN.1 变种自然进化的亚系,而不是重组菌株,并且没有证据表明比其他 Omicron 系更严重。症状与其他 Omicron 亚变体相似。该州本月报告了 16 例病例,截至 7 月 16 日已发现 12 例。世界卫生组织正在监测该菌株,安得拉邦政府已采取预防措施,包括专门的医院病房和检测套件,并表示该州已做好充分准备,应对任何感染上升。
查看新闻五要素
时间
文章发表于2026-07-19。在收集样本并送去基因组测序时,在四例 Covid-19 病例中检测到 Omicron RF.5 亚变种,文章指出,本月到目前为止,该州已报告了 16 例病例,其中 12 例是在 7 月 16 日之前检测到的。
地点与对象
印度安得拉邦。受影响的人群包括卡达帕地区的 4 例 Covid-19 病例。该州医疗卫生部门当局参与其中。
事件
安得拉邦报告的 4 例 Covid-19 病例中检测到了 SARS-CoV-2 的 Omicron RF.5 亚变种。该州医疗和卫生部门当局以及浦那国家病毒学研究所 (NIV) 证实了这一点,该研究所进行了基因组测序。
规模、影响与风险
已检测到 4 例 Omicron RF.5 亚变种。本月到目前为止,该州已报告 16 例 Covid-19 病例,其中 7 月 16 日之前发现了 12 例。没有科学证据表明该菌株比其他 Omicron 谱系更严重。症状与其他 Omicron 亚变种相关的症状仍然相似。
应对、状态与不确定性
世界卫生组织 (WHO) 正在监测 RF.5,作为其 SARS-CoV-2 常规基因组监测的一部分。州政府已采取预防措施,包括在教学医院准备专用病房,并储备检测试剂盒和其他必要设备。卫生官员表示,无需恐慌,该州已做好充分准备应对任何感染病例的增加。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Omicron RF.5 sub-variant detected in four Covid-19 cases reported in Andhra Pradesh

News Brief
On July 19, 2026, The Times of India reported that the Omicron RF.5 sub-variant was detected in four Covid-19 cases in Kadapa district, Andhra Pradesh. The samples were sent to the National Institute of Virology in Pune for genome sequencing. Health officials clarified that RF.5 is a naturally evolved sub-lineage of the JN.1 variant, not a recombinant strain, and has no evidence of being more severe than other Omicron lineages. Symptoms remain similar to other Omicron sub-variants. The state has reported 16 cases this month, with 12 detected by July 16. The World Health Organization is monitoring the strain, and the Andhra Pradesh government has implemented precautionary measures, including dedicated hospital wards and testing kits, stating the state is fully prepared to manage any infection rise.
View five news elements
Time
The article was published on 2026-07-19. The event of detecting the Omicron RF.5 sub-variant in four Covid-19 cases occurred when samples were collected and sent for genome sequencing, with the article noting that the state had reported 16 cases so far this month, of which 12 were detected till July 16.
Location and population
Andhra Pradesh, India. The affected population includes four Covid-19 cases in Kadapa district. The state's medical and health department authorities are involved.
Event
The Omicron RF.5 sub-variant of SARS-CoV-2 has been detected in four Covid-19 cases reported in Andhra Pradesh. This is confirmed by the state's medical and health department authorities and the National Institute of Virology (NIV), Pune, which performed genome sequencing.
Scale, impact and risk
Four cases of the Omicron RF.5 sub-variant have been detected. The state has reported 16 Covid-19 cases so far this month, with 12 detected till July 16. There is no scientific evidence to suggest that the strain is more severe than other Omicron lineages. Symptoms remain similar to those associated with other Omicron sub-variants.
Response, status and uncertainty
The World Health Organization (WHO) is monitoring RF.5 as part of its routine genomic surveillance of SARS-CoV-2. The state government has taken precautionary measures, including readying dedicated wards in teaching hospitals and stocking up on testing kits and other essential equipment. Health officials have stated that there is no need to panic and that the state remains fully prepared to deal with any rise in infections.

COVID-19 - 大英百科全书

COVID-19 - Encyclopedia Britannica
新闻简报截至 2026 年 7 月,由 SARS-CoV-2 引起的 COVID-19 仍然是全球公共卫生问题,距离在中国武汉首次发现已近七年。这一流行病已导致全球数百万人死亡,对老年人和慢性病患者造成严重影响。高度传播的变体,例如 Omicron 及其亚系(例如 BA.4、BA.5、XBB.1.5)继续推动传播,表现出增强的感染和逃避免疫力的能力。虽然尚无明确的治疗方法,但抗病毒药物和单克隆抗体等治疗方法以及 2020 年底推出的广泛使用的疫苗已经减轻了严重的后果。预防策略,包括加强疫苗接种、戴口罩和保持社交距离,仍然至关重要。幸存者,尤其是住院患者,仍存在长期并发症,包括神经系统症状和心理影响。大流行的轨迹是由病毒的进化决定的,这凸显了持续监测和适应性公共卫生措施的必要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026年7月16日;活动日期:2019-2026(持续的大流行)。
地点与对象
全球;首次在中国武汉(2019)检测到;奥密克戎变异株首次在博茨瓦纳(2021年11月)检测到。
事件
COVID-19 是一种由 SARS-CoV-2 引起的高度传染性呼吸道疾病,被《大英百科全书》编辑报道为大流行病。它被描述为一个历史性的、持续的事件,具有记录的变异和长期的健康影响。
规模、影响与风险
在多年的大流行期间,全世界有数百万人死亡;危险人群包括老年人和慢性病患者。变体(例如 Omicron、BA.4、BA.5、XBB.1.5)表现出更高的传播性和免疫逃逸能力。长期影响包括神经系统症状、谵妄和严重病例恢复时间延长。
应对、状态与不确定性
没有治愈方法;治疗包括抗病毒药物(例如瑞德西韦、莫努匹拉韦、利托那韦/尼马瑞韦)、单克隆抗体和甲磺酸卡莫司他。疫苗(2020 年末上市)对于治疗严重疾病非常有效;建议加强剂量。预防措施包括保持社交距离、消毒、戴口罩、自我隔离和检疫。新兴变种推动了持续传播;截至 2026 年 7 月,大流行状态仍在持续。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

COVID-19 - Encyclopedia Britannica

News Brief
COVID-19, caused by SARS-CoV-2, remains a global public health concern as of July 2026, nearly seven years after its initial detection in Wuhan, China. The pandemic has resulted in millions of deaths worldwide, with severe impacts on older adults and those with chronic conditions. Highly transmissible variants such as Omicron and its sublineages (e.g., BA.4, BA.5, XBB.1.5) continue to drive transmission, exhibiting enhanced abilities to infect and evade immunity. While no definitive cure exists, treatments like antivirals and monoclonal antibodies, alongside widely available vaccines introduced in late 2020, have mitigated severe outcomes. Prevention strategies, including vaccination boosters, mask-wearing, and social distancing, remain critical. Long-term complications, including neurological symptoms and psychological effects, persist among survivors, particularly those hospitalized. The pandemic’s trajectory is shaped by the virus’s evolution, underscoring the need for sustained surveillance and adaptive public health measures.
View five news elements
Time
Publication date: 16 July 2026; event date: 2019–2026 (ongoing pandemic).
Location and population
Global; first detected in Wuhan, China (2019); variants first detected in Botswana (Omicron, November 2021).
Event
COVID-19 is a highly contagious respiratory illness caused by SARS-CoV-2, reported as a pandemic by Britannica Editors. It is characterized as a historical, ongoing event with documented variants and long-term health impacts.
Scale, impact and risk
Millions of deaths worldwide during the multiyear pandemic; risk groups include older adults and persons with chronic illness. Variants (e.g., Omicron, BA.4, BA.5, XBB.1.5) exhibit increased transmissibility and immune escape. Long-term effects include neurological symptoms, delirium, and prolonged recovery for severe cases.
Response, status and uncertainty
No cure exists; treatments include antivirals (e.g., remdesivir, molnupiravir, ritonavir/nirmatrelvir), monoclonal antibodies, and camostat mesilate. Vaccines (available late 2020) are highly effective against severe illness; booster doses recommended. Prevention measures include social distancing, disinfection, face masks, self-isolation, and quarantine. Ongoing spread fueled by emerging variants; pandemic status ongoing as of July 2026.

隐藏的途径驱动了 COVID-19 感染:研究 - 拉筹伯大学

Hidden pathway drives COVID-19 infections: study - La Trobe University
新闻简报拉筹伯大学和 WEHI 研究人员于 2026 年 7 月 15 日在《自然通讯》上发表了一项研究,揭示 SARS-CoV-2 可以隐藏在凋亡小体 (ApoBD) 内,感染免疫细胞并引发破坏性肺部炎症。研究发现,病毒绕过通常的 ACE2 受体进入途径,在人体的“清理”过程中在免疫细胞之间移动。通过使用 T 型钙通道阻滞剂阻​​断这些病毒感染的细胞碎片的形成,研究人员能够减少肺损伤、病毒传播和有害炎症。这一发现也可能适用于流感和呼吸道合胞病毒等其他呼吸道病毒,提供新的治疗途径来补充现有的抗病毒药物。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于 2026 年 7 月 15 日发表在《自然通讯》(N7、N8)上。
地点与对象
该研究由拉筹伯大学和 WEHI 研究人员 (N8) 进行。
事件
拉筹伯大学和 WEHI 研究人员在《自然通讯》上发表了一项研究,揭示 SARS-CoV-2 可以隐藏在凋亡小体 (ApoBD) 内,感染免疫细胞并引发破坏性肺部炎症 (N8)。
规模、影响与风险
该研究确定了 SARS-CoV-2 以前未知的绕过 ACE2 受体的传播途径,这可能解释了 COVID-19 如何感染免疫细胞并导致严重的肺组织损伤 (N8)。研究结果表明,流感和 RSV 等呼吸道病毒感染可能涉及类似的机制 (N8)。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究发表在《自然通讯》(N8) 上并经过同行评审。作者提出,重新利用现有的 T 型钙通道阻滞剂可以通过阻止病毒感染的凋亡小体 (N8) 的形成来减少肺损伤和病毒传播。治疗流感和 RSV 的影响仍在研究中 (N8)。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Hidden pathway drives COVID-19 infections: study - La Trobe University

News Brief
La Trobe University and WEHI researchers published a study in Nature Communications on 15 July 2026 revealing that SARS-CoV-2 can hide inside apoptotic bodies (ApoBDs) to infect immune cells and trigger damaging lung inflammation. The study found that the virus bypasses the usual ACE2 receptor entry route, moving between immune cells during the body's 'clean-up' process. By blocking the formation of these virus-infected cell fragments using T-type calcium channel blockers, the researchers were able to reduce lung damage, viral spread, and harmful inflammation. This discovery may also apply to other respiratory viruses like influenza and RSV, offering new therapeutic pathways that complement existing anti-viral drugs.
View five news elements
Time
The study was published in Nature Communications on 15 July 2026 (N7, N8).
Location and population
The research was conducted by La Trobe University and WEHI researchers (N8).
Event
La Trobe University and WEHI researchers published a study in Nature Communications revealing that SARS-CoV-2 can hide inside apoptotic bodies (ApoBDs) to infect immune cells and trigger damaging lung inflammation (N8).
Scale, impact and risk
The study identifies a previously unknown transmission pathway for SARS-CoV-2 that bypasses ACE2 receptors, potentially explaining how COVID-19 infects immune cells and drives severe lung tissue damage (N8). The findings suggest that respiratory viral infections like influenza and RSV may involve similar mechanisms (N8).
Response, status and uncertainty
The study is published and peer-reviewed in Nature Communications (N8). The authors propose that repurposing existing T-type calcium channel blockers could reduce lung damage and viral spread by blocking the formation of virus-infected apoptotic bodies (N8). The implications for treating influenza and RSV remain under investigation (N8).

COVID-19 加强剂比较显示 mRNA 疫苗的抗体水平更高 - 药物主题

COVID-19 Booster Comparison Shows Higher Antibody Levels With mRNA Vaccine - Drug Topics
新闻简报澳大利亚的一项随机对照试验在成人中比较了第四剂二价 mRNA 疫苗 (Moderna) 和基于蛋白质的疫苗 (Novavax)。在 12 个月的时间里,mRNA 疫苗产生了更高、更持久的抗体反应,尽管两种疫苗都表现出类似的针对 SARS-CoV-2 感染的保护作用。与对照组相比,两个疫苗组的感染率均较低,六个月后功效下降。所有感染均为轻度,不良事件并不常见,其中两起严重事件可能与疫苗接种有关。该研究发表在《感染杂志》上,首次对这些疫苗类型之间的长期免疫原性和功效进行直接比较。（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
这项随机对照试验从 2023 年 2 月开始进行,并进行了 12 个月的随访,研究发表在《感染杂志》上。该新闻报道发布于2026年7月18日。
地点与对象
澳大利亚;至少 6 个月前接受过第三剂 COVID-19 疫苗的 18 岁及以上成年人(二价 mRNA 疫苗 n = 177,蛋白质疫苗 n = 176,非随机年龄匹配对照组 n = 143)。
事件
一项随机对照试验,比较第四剂二价 mRNA 疫苗 (Moderna) 与蛋白质疫苗 (Novavax) 在成人中的免疫原性和功效。该研究被描述为首次对这些疫苗类型之间的长期免疫原性和功效进行直接比较。
规模、影响与风险
Over 12 months, SARS-CoV-2 infection occurred in 68.1% (94 of 138) of the control group, compared with 43.0% (74 of 172) in the Moderna group and 47.0% (77 of 164) in the Novavax group. Vaccine efficacy against combined documented and undocumented infection was 33% (95% CI, 17%-46%) for Moderna and 29% (95% CI, 13%-42%) for Novavax over the full 12 months. Efficacy was higher in the first 6 months (61% for Moderna, 50% for Novavax) and declined between 6 and 12 months (12% for Moderna, 17% for Novavax). All infections were mild and did not require hospitalization.
应对、状态与不确定性
该试验是一项随机对照试验,随访期为 12 个月。研究作者将其描述为对第四剂 mRNA 疫苗和蛋白质 COVID-19 疫苗之间长期免疫原性和功效的首次直接比较。该研究结果发表在《感染杂志》上,基于一项对照研究设计,使用血液样本测量结合 IgG 抗体、替代中和抗体和干扰素伽马释放测定 (IGRA) 反应。该研究是在澳大利亚 JN.1 感染浪潮期间(2023 年 11 月至 2024 年 6 月)进行的。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

COVID-19 Booster Comparison Shows Higher Antibody Levels With mRNA Vaccine - Drug Topics

News Brief
A randomized controlled trial in Australia compared a fourth dose of a bivalent mRNA vaccine (Moderna) with a protein-based vaccine (Novavax) in adults. Over 12 months, the mRNA vaccine generated higher and more durable antibody responses, though both vaccines showed similar protection against SARS-CoV-2 infection. Infection rates were lower in both vaccine groups compared to the control group, with efficacy declining after six months. All infections were mild, and adverse events were uncommon, with two serious events possibly related to vaccination. The study, published in the Journal of Infection, is the first direct comparison of long-term immunogenicity and efficacy between these vaccine types.
View five news elements
Time
The randomized controlled trial was conducted from February 2023 through 12-month follow-up visits, with the study published in the Journal of Infection. The news report was published on July 18, 2026.
Location and population
Australia; adults 18 years and older who had received a third COVID-19 vaccine dose at least 6 months earlier (n = 177 for bivalent mRNA vaccine, n = 176 for protein-based vaccine, and n = 143 for a nonrandomized age-matched control group).
Event
A randomized controlled trial comparing the immunogenicity and efficacy of a fourth dose of a bivalent mRNA vaccine (Moderna) versus a protein-based vaccine (Novavax) in adults. The study is described as the first direct comparison of long-term immunogenicity and efficacy between these vaccine types.
Scale, impact and risk
Over 12 months, SARS-CoV-2 infection occurred in 68.1% (94 of 138) of the control group, compared with 43.0% (74 of 172) in the Moderna group and 47.0% (77 of 164) in the Novavax group. Vaccine efficacy against combined documented and undocumented infection was 33% (95% CI, 17%-46%) for Moderna and 29% (95% CI, 13%-42%) for Novavax over the full 12 months. Efficacy was higher in the first 6 months (61% for Moderna, 50% for Novavax) and declined between 6 and 12 months (12% for Moderna, 17% for Novavax). All infections were mild and did not require hospitalization.
Response, status and uncertainty
The trial is a randomized controlled trial with a 12-month follow-up period. The study authors describe it as the first direct comparison of long-term immunogenicity and efficacy between an mRNA and a protein COVID-19 vaccine given as a fourth dose. The findings are published in the Journal of Infection and are based on a controlled study design with blood samples measuring binding IgG antibodies, surrogate neutralizing antibodies, and interferon-gamma release assay (IGRA) responses. The study was conducted during a JN.1 infection wave in Australia (November 2023 to June 2024).

COVID-19 检测在假期前加强 - 林肯县新闻

COVID-19 Testing Ramps Up Before Holidays - The Lincoln County News
新闻简报Lincoln County, Maine, reported a 5.36% COVID-19 test positivity rate for the week ending December 19, 2022, down from 9.92% the prior week, with 15 of 281 tests returning positive. Local health officials noted that community transmission had risen to 'substantial' while the county’s overall impact level remained 'low.' Cumulatively, Lincoln County has recorded 6,693 cases (5,581 confirmed, 1,112 probable) and 42 deaths since March 2020, with hospitalizations increasing to 113. Statewide, Maine reported 3,237 new cases and two additional deaths during the same period. Vaccination coverage in the county stands at 80.75% for full primary series and 266,688.80 doses per 100,000 residents when including boosters. LincolnHealth continues to promote testing, vaccination, and staying home when sick as key prevention measures.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026-07-16。事件期间:2022年12月13–19日(周报)。
地点与对象
美国缅因州林肯县;人口34,634。检测和疫苗接种数据指的是县居民。
事件
LincolnHealth 和缅因州 CDC 报告的 COVID-19 活动每周更新。包括检测结果、疫苗接种覆盖率和社区层面的指标。
规模、影响与风险
进行了 281 项检测,其中 15 例呈阳性(阳性率为 5.36%,低于 9.92%)。全县累计病例:6,693 例(确诊 5,581 例,疑似病例 1,112 例)。住院人数:113 人(增加 1 人)。全州范围:304,797 例病例(新增 3,237 例),住院治疗 6,463 例(新增 34 例),死亡 2,779 例(新增 2 例)。社区传播升级为“大量”,而县级影响仍为“低”。
应对、状态与不确定性
LincolnHealth 敦促接种疫苗(6 个月以上)和流感/COVID-19 加强疫苗;促进出现症状或暴露时进行检测;建议生病时呆在家里。MaineHealth 维护着一个 COVID-19 信息数据库。数据截至 2022 年 12 月 19 日。所提供的证据中没有报告未解决的不确定性。

Vaccination coverage in the county stands at 80.75% for full primary series and 266,688.80 doses per 100,000 residents when including boosters.
